<dyad-write path="types.ts" description="">
export enum ChoreStatus {
  Incomplete = 'INCOMPLETE',
  PendingApproval = 'PENDING_APPROVAL',
  Completed = 'COMPLETED',
  RetryRequested = 'RETRY_REQUESTED',
}

export enum ChoreRecurrence {
  None = 'NONE',
  Daily = 'DAILY',
  Weekly = 'WEEKLY',
  Monthly = 'MONTHLY',
  Yearly = 'YEARLY',
}

export type ChoreScheduleType = 'SAME_DAY' | 'FIXED_SCHEDULE' | null;

export interface ChoreSchedule {
  type: 'DEADLINE' | 'DAY_OF_WEEK' | null;
  value: string;
  scheduleType: ChoreScheduleType;
  varianceDays: number | null;
}

export interface Chore {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  points: number;
  status: ChoreStatus;
  requiresApproval: boolean;
  recurrence: ChoreRecurrence;
  schedule?: ChoreSchedule;
  isExtraChore?: boolean;
  description?: string;
  assignedTo?: number;
  streak?: number;
  lastCompletedDate?: string;
  retryAllowed?: boolean;
}

export interface Reward {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  points: number;
}

export enum View {
  Chores = 'CHORES',
  Rewards = 'REWARDS',
  Requests = 'REQUESTS',
  Users = 'USERS',
  ActivityLog = 'ACTIVITY_LOG',
}

export type UserRole = 'parent' | 'child';

export interface User {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  role: UserRole;
  avatar: string | null;
  points: number;
  pin?: string;
}

export enum PointRequestStatus {
  Pending = 'PENDING',
  Approved = 'APPROVED',
  Denied = 'DENIED',
}

export interface PointRequest {
  id: number;
  userId: number;
  description: string;
  points: number;
  status: PointRequestStatus;
}

export interface Notification {
  id: number;
  message: string;
  timestamp: number;
  read: boolean;
  targetRole: UserRole;
}

export enum ActivityEventType {
  CHORE_COMPLETED = 'CHORE_COMPLETED',
  CHORE_APPROVED = 'CHORE_APPROVED',
  CHORE_DECLINED = 'CHORE_DECLINED',
  CHORE_MISSED = 'CHORE_MISSED',
  CHORE_RETRIED = 'CHORE_RETRIED',
  REWARD_REDEEMED = 'REWARD_REDEEMED',
  POINTS_AWARDED = 'POINTS_AWARDED',
  POINTS_DEDUCTED = 'POINTS_DEDUCTED',
  CHORE_CREATED = 'CHORE_CREATED',
  CHORE_EDITED = 'CHORE_EDITED',
}

export interface ActivityEvent {
  id: number;
  type: ActivityEventType;
  userId: number;
  userName: string;
  choreName?: string;
  rewardName?: string;
  points: number;
  timestamp: number;
  description?: string;
}

export interface ChildPIN {
  userId: number;
  pin: string;
}

</dyad-write>

<dyad-write path="components/LoginScreen.tsx" description="">
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { User } from '../types';
import { LockIcon, ArrowLeftIcon, QuestionMarkCircleIcon, XCircleIcon } from './icons';

interface LoginScreenProps {
  users: User[];
  onLogin: (userId: number) =&gt; void;
  onParentLogin: () =&gt; void;
}

const LoginScreen: React.FC&lt;LoginScreenProps&gt; = ({ users, onLogin, onParentLogin }) =&gt; {
  const [pin, setPin] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [showForgot, setShowForgot] = useState(false);
  const childUsers = users.filter(u =&gt; u.role === 'child');

  const handleKeyPress = (digit: string) =&gt; {
    if (pin.length &lt; 8) {
      setPin(prev =&gt; prev + digit);
      setError('');
    }
  };

  const handleBackspace = () =&gt; {
    setPin(prev =&gt; prev.slice(0, -1));
    setError('');
  };

  const handleSubmit = () =&gt; {
    if (pin.length &lt; 4) {
      setError('PIN must be 4-8 digits');
      return;
    }
    const matchedChild = childUsers.find(child =&gt; child.pin === pin);
    if (matchedChild) {
      onLogin(matchedChild.id);
      setPin('');
      setError('');
    } else {
      setError('Invalid PIN');
    }
  };

  return (
    &lt;div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-sky-100 to-indigo-100 p-4"&gt;
      &lt;div className="w-full max-w-sm"&gt;
        &lt;div className="text-center mb-8"&gt;
          &lt;div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-20 h-20 bg-sky-500 rounded-full mb-4 shadow-lg"&gt;
            &lt;LockIcon className="w-10 h-10 text-white" /&gt;
          &lt;/div&gt;
          &lt;h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-800"&gt;Chore Champ&lt;/h1&gt;
          &lt;p className="text-slate-600 mt-2"&gt;Enter your PIN to continue&lt;/p&gt;
        &lt;/div&gt;

        &lt;div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-6 mb-6"&gt;
          &lt;div className="flex justify-center mb-6"&gt;
            &lt;div className="flex space-x-3"&gt;
              {[...Array(8)].map((_, i) =&gt; (
                &lt;div
                  key={i}
                  className={`w-4 h-4 rounded-full transition-all duration-200 ${
                    i &lt; pin.length ? 'bg-sky-500 scale-110' : 'bg-slate-200'
                  }`}
                /&gt;
              ))}
            &lt;/div&gt;
          &lt;/div&gt;

          {error &amp;&amp; (
            &lt;div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 text-red-600 rounded-lg text-sm text-center font-medium"&gt;
              {error}
            &lt;/div&gt;
          )}

          &lt;div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3"&gt;
            {['1','2','3','4','5','6','7','8','9'].map(digit =&gt; (
              &lt;button
                key={digit}
                onClick={() =&gt; handleKeyPress(digit)}
                className="h-14 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-xl font-bold text-slate-700 hover:bg-sky-100 active:bg-sky-200 transition-colors shadow-sm"
              &gt;
                {digit}
              &lt;/button&gt;
            ))}
            &lt;button
              onClick={() =&gt; setShowForgot(true)}
              className="h-14 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 text-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-100 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center"
            &gt;
              &lt;QuestionMarkCircleIcon className="w-7 h-7" /&gt;
            &lt;/button&gt;
            &lt;button
              onClick={() =&gt; handleKeyPress('0')}
              className="h-14 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-xl font-bold text-slate-700 hover:bg-sky-100 active:bg-sky-200 transition-colors shadow-sm"
            &gt;
              0
            &lt;/button&gt;
            &lt;button
              onClick={handleBackspace}
              className="h-14 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-red-100 hover:text-red-600 active:bg-red-200 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm"
            &gt;
              &lt;svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" strokeWidth={1.5} stroke="currentColor" className="w-7 h-7"&gt;
                &lt;path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M12 9.75L14.25 12 12 14.25M16.5 12H3m14.25-2.25L19.5 12l-1.75 2.25" /&gt;
              &lt;/svg&gt;
            &lt;/button&gt;
          &lt;/div&gt;

          &lt;button
            onClick={handleSubmit}
            className="w-full mt-4 h-14 rounded-xl bg-sky-500 text-white text-lg font-bold hover:bg-sky-600 active:bg-sky-700 transition-colors shadow-md"
          &gt;
            Enter
          &lt;/button&gt;
        &lt;/div&gt;

        &lt;button
          onClick={onParentLogin}
          className="w-full text-center text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700 font-medium text-sm py-3"
        &gt;
          &lt;ArrowLeftIcon className="w-4 h-4 inline mr-1" /&gt;
          I'll login as parent
        &lt;/button&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;

      {showForgot &amp;&amp; (
        &lt;div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 z-50 flex justify-center items-center p-4" onClick={() =&gt; setShowForgot(false)}&gt;
          &lt;div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-6 w-full max-w-sm" onClick={e =&gt; e.stopPropagation()}&gt;
            &lt;div className="text-center"&gt;
              &lt;XCircleIcon className="w-12 h-12 text-amber-500 mx-auto mb-3" /&gt;
              &lt;h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800 mb-2"&gt;Forgot PIN?&lt;/h3&gt;
              &lt;p className="text-slate-600 mb-4"&gt;
                Ask your parent to check or reset your PIN in the &lt;strong&gt;Family&lt;/strong&gt; tab.
              &lt;/p&gt;
              &lt;button
                onClick={() =&gt; setShowForgot(false)}
                className="w-full bg-sky-500 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-sky-600 transition-colors"
              &gt;
                Got it
              &lt;/button&gt;
            &lt;/div&gt;
          &lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;/div&gt;
      )}
    &lt;/div&gt;
  );
};

export default LoginScreen;

<dyad-write path="components/ActivityLog.tsx" description="">
import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { ActivityEvent, ActivityEventType, User, UserRole } from '../types';
import { CheckCircleIcon, XCircleIcon, ClockIcon, RefreshIcon, GiftIcon, StarIcon, ArrowDownIcon, PlusIcon, PencilIcon, ChevronDownIcon } from './icons';

interface ActivityLogProps {
  events: ActivityEvent[];
  users: User[];
}

type EventFilterType = 'ALL' | 'CHORE' | 'REWARD' | 'POINTS';

const eventConfig: Record&lt;ActivityEventType, { icon: React.ReactNode; color: string; label: string }&gt; = {
  [ActivityEventType.CHORE_COMPLETED]: { icon: &lt;CheckCircleIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;, color: 'text-emerald-500 bg-emerald-50', label: 'Chore Completed' },
  [ActivityEventType.CHORE_APPROVED]: { icon: &lt;CheckCircleIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;, color: 'text-emerald-600 bg-emerald-100', label: 'Chore Approved' },
  [ActivityEventType.CHORE_DECLINED]: { icon: &lt;XCircleIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;, color: 'text-red-500 bg-red-50', label: 'Chore Declined' },
  [ActivityEventType.CHORE_MISSED]: { icon: &lt;ClockIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;, color: 'text-amber-500 bg-amber-50', label: 'Chore Missed' },
  [ActivityEventType.CHORE_RETRIED]: { icon: &lt;RefreshIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;, color: 'text-blue-500 bg-blue-50', label: 'Chore Retried' },
  [ActivityEventType.REWARD_REDEEMED]: { icon: &lt;GiftIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;, color: 'text-purple-500 bg-purple-50', label: 'Reward Redeemed' },
  [ActivityEventType.POINTS_AWARDED]: { icon: &lt;StarIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;, color: 'text-amber-500 bg-amber-50', label: 'Points Awarded' },
  [ActivityEventType.POINTS_DEDUCTED]: { icon: &lt;ArrowDownIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;, color: 'text-rose-500 bg-rose-50', label: 'Points Deducted' },
  [ActivityEventType.CHORE_CREATED]: { icon: &lt;PlusIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;, color: 'text-sky-500 bg-sky-50', label: 'Chore Created' },
  [ActivityEventType.CHORE_EDITED]: { icon: &lt;PencilIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;, color: 'text-slate-500 bg-slate-100', label: 'Chore Edited' },
};

const getEventTitle = (event: ActivityEvent): string =&gt; {
  switch (event.type) {
    case ActivityEventType.CHORE_COMPLETED:
      return `Completed "${event.choreName}"`;
    case ActivityEventType.CHORE_APPROVED:
      return `"${event.choreName}" approved`;
    case ActivityEventType.CHORE_DECLINED:
      return `"${event.choreName}" declined`;
    case ActivityEventType.CHORE_MISSED:
      return `Missed "${event.choreName}"`;
    case ActivityEventType.CHORE_RETRIED:
      return `Retrying "${event.choreName}"`;
    case ActivityEventType.REWARD_REDEEMED:
      return `Redeemed "${event.rewardName}"`;
    case ActivityEventType.POINTS_AWARDED:
      return event.description || 'Points awarded';
    case ActivityEventType.POINTS_DEDUCTED:
      return event.description || 'Points deducted';
    case ActivityEventType.CHORE_CREATED:
      return `Created "${event.choreName}"`;
    case ActivityEventType.CHORE_EDITED:
      return `Edited "${event.choreName}"`;
    default:
      return event.description || 'Unknown event';
  }
};

const matchesFilter = (event: ActivityEvent, filterType: EventFilterType): boolean =&gt; {
  if (filterType === 'ALL') return true;
  switch (filterType) {
    case 'CHORE':
      return [
        ActivityEventType.CHORE_COMPLETED,
        ActivityEventType.CHORE_APPROVED,
        ActivityEventType.CHORE_DECLINED,
        ActivityEventType.CHORE_MISSED,
        ActivityEventType.CHORE_RETRIED,
        ActivityEventType.CHORE_CREATED,
        ActivityEventType.CHORE_EDITED,
      ].includes(event.type);
    case 'REWARD':
      return event.type === ActivityEventType.REWARD_REDEEMED;
    case 'POINTS':
      return event.type === ActivityEventType.POINTS_AWARDED || event.type === ActivityEventType.POINTS_DEDUCTED;
    default:
      return true;
  }
};

const ActivityLog: React.FC&lt;ActivityLogProps&gt; = ({ events, users }) =&gt; {
  const [childFilter, setChildFilter] = useState&lt;number | 'all'&gt;('all');
  const [typeFilter, setTypeFilter] = useState&lt;EventFilterType&gt;('ALL');
  const [showChildDropdown, setShowChildDropdown] = useState(false);
  const [showTypeDropdown, setShowTypeDropdown] = useState(false);

  const childUsers = useMemo(() =&gt; users.filter(u =&gt; u.role === UserRole.child), [users]);

  const filteredEvents = useMemo(() =&gt; {
    return events
      .filter(event =&gt; childFilter === 'all' || event.userId === childFilter)
      .filter(event =&gt; matchesFilter(event, typeFilter))
      .slice(0, 100);
  }, [events, childFilter, typeFilter]);

  return (
    &lt;div&gt;
      &lt;div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 mb-6"&gt;
        &lt;div className="relative"&gt;
          &lt;button
            onClick={() =&gt; { setShowChildDropdown(!showChildDropdown); setShowTypeDropdown(false); }}
            className="w-full sm:w-auto flex items-center justify-between space-x-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50"
          &gt;
            &lt;span&gt;
              {childFilter === 'all' ? 'All Children' : childUsers.find(c =&gt; c.id === childFilter)?.name || 'All Children'}
            &lt;/span&gt;
            &lt;ChevronDownIcon className="w-4 h-4" /&gt;
          &lt;/button&gt;
          {showChildDropdown &amp;&amp; (
            &lt;div className="absolute top-full left-0 mt-1 w-48 bg-white rounded-lg shadow-lg border border-slate-200 z-30"&gt;
              &lt;button
                onClick={() =&gt; { setChildFilter('all'); setShowChildDropdown(false); }}
                className={`w-full text-left px-4 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-slate-50 first:rounded-t-lg last:rounded-b-lg ${childFilter === 'all' ? 'bg-sky-50 text-sky-700 font-semibold' : 'text-slate-700'}`}
              &gt;
                All Children
              &lt;/button&gt;
              {childUsers.map(child =&gt; (
                &lt;button
                  key={child.id}
                  onClick={() =&gt; { setChildFilter(child.id); setShowChildDropdown(false); }}
                  className={`w-full text-left px-4 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-slate-50 first:rounded-t-lg last:rounded-b-lg ${childFilter === child.id ? 'bg-sky-50 text-sky-700 font-semibold' : 'text-slate-700'}`}
                &gt;
                  {child.name}
                &lt;/button&gt;
              ))}
            &lt;/div&gt;
          )}
        &lt;/div&gt;

        &lt;div className="relative"&gt;
          &lt;button
            onClick={() =&gt; { setShowTypeDropdown(!showTypeDropdown); setShowChildDropdown(false); }}
            className="w-full sm:w-auto flex items-center justify-between space-x-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50"
          &gt;
            &lt;span&gt;
              {typeFilter === 'ALL' ? 'All Events' : typeFilter === 'CHORE' ? 'Chore Events' : typeFilter === 'REWARD' ? 'Reward Events' : 'Points Events'}
            &lt;/span&gt;
            &lt;ChevronDownIcon className="w-4 h-4" /&gt;
          &lt;/button&gt;
          {showTypeDropdown &amp;&amp; (
            &lt;div className="absolute top-full left-0 mt-1 w-48 bg-white rounded-lg shadow-lg border border-slate-200 z-30"&gt;
              {(['ALL', 'CHORE', 'REWARD', 'POINTS'] as EventFilterType[]).map(t =&gt; (
                &lt;button
                  key={t}
                  onClick={() =&gt; { setTypeFilter(t); setShowTypeDropdown(false); }}
                  className={`w-full text-left px-4 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-slate-50 first:rounded-t-lg last:rounded-b-lg ${typeFilter === t ? 'bg-sky-50 text-sky-700 font-semibold' : 'text-slate-700'}`}
                &gt;
                  {t === 'ALL' ? 'All Events' : t === 'CHORE' ? 'Chore Events' : t === 'REWARD' ? 'Reward Events' : 'Points Events'}
                &lt;/button&gt;
              ))}
            &lt;/div&gt;
          )}
        &lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;

      {filteredEvents.length === 0 ? (
        &lt;div className="text-center py-12 text-slate-500"&gt;
          &lt;ListIcon className="w-12 h-12 mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" /&gt;
          &lt;p className="font-semibold"&gt;No events found&lt;/p&gt;
          &lt;p className="text-sm"&gt;Try adjusting your filters&lt;/p&gt;
        &lt;/div&gt;
      ) : (
        &lt;div className="space-y-3"&gt;
          {filteredEvents.map(event =&gt; {
            const config = eventConfig[event.type];
            const user = users.find(u =&gt; u.id === event.userId);
            return (
              &lt;div key={event.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-slate-100 p-4 flex items-center space-x-4"&gt;
                &lt;div className={`flex-shrink-0 w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center ${config.color}`}&gt;
                  {config.icon}
                &lt;/div&gt;
                &lt;div className="flex-grow min-w-0"&gt;
                  &lt;p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800"&gt;{getEventTitle(event)}&lt;/p&gt;
                  &lt;p className="text-xs text-slate-500"&gt;{config.label}&lt;/p&gt;
                &lt;/div&gt;
                &lt;div className="text-right flex-shrink-0"&gt;
                  &lt;p className="text-xs text-slate-500"&gt;{user?.name}&lt;/p&gt;
                  &lt;p className="text-xs text-slate-400"&gt;{new Date(event.timestamp).toLocaleString()}&lt;/p&gt;
                  {event.points !== 0 &amp;&amp; (
                    &lt;p className={`text-sm font-bold ${event.points &gt; 0 ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-red-600'}`}&gt;
                      {event.points &gt; 0 ? '+' : ''}{event.points} pts
                    &lt;/p&gt;
                  )}
                &lt;/div&gt;
              &lt;/div&gt;
            );
          })}
        &lt;/div&gt;
      )}
    &lt;/div&gt;
  );
};

export default ActivityLog;

<dyad-write path="components/ChoreCard.tsx" description="">
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Chore, ChoreStatus, ChoreRecurrence, User, ChoreSchedule } from '../types';
import { StarIcon, CheckCircleIcon, RefreshIcon, InformationCircleIcon, PencilIcon, FireIcon, ShieldCheckIcon, XCircleIcon, CalendarIcon, ClockIcon } from './icons';

interface ChoreCardProps {
  chore: Chore;
  onStateChange: (id: number, newStatus: ChoreStatus) =&gt; void;
  currentUser: User;
  onEdit: (chore: Chore) =&gt; void;
  onOverride: (id: number) =&gt; void;
}

const RecurrenceBadge: React.FC&lt;{ recurrence: ChoreRecurrence; schedule?: ChoreSchedule }&gt; = ({ recurrence, schedule }) =&gt; {
  if (recurrence === ChoreRecurrence.None &amp;&amp; !schedule) return null;

  const getLabel = () =&gt; {
    switch (recurrence) {
      case ChoreRecurrence.Daily: return 'Daily';
      case ChoreRecurrence.Weekly: return 'Weekly';
      case ChoreRecurrence.Monthly:
        return `Monthly ${schedule?.scheduleType === 'SAME_DAY' ? '(Same Day)' : '(Fixed)'}`;
      case ChoreRecurrence.Yearly:
        return `Yearly ${schedule?.scheduleType === 'SAME_DAY' ? '(Same Date)' : '(Fixed)'}`;
      default:
        if (schedule?.type === 'DEADLINE') return `Due ${schedule.value}`;
        if (schedule?.type === 'DAY_OF_WEEK') return `Every ${schedule.value}`;
        return '';
    }
  };

  const getIcon = () =&gt; {
    if (recurrence === ChoreRecurrence.Monthly || recurrence === ChoreRecurrence.Yearly || schedule?.type === 'DEADLINE') {
      return &lt;CalendarIcon className="w-3 h-3" /&gt;;
    }
    return &lt;RefreshIcon className="w-3 h-3" /&gt;;
  };

  const getColorClass = () =&gt; {
    switch (recurrence) {
      case ChoreRecurrence.Daily: return 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700';
      case ChoreRecurrence.Weekly: return 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700';
      case ChoreRecurrence.Monthly:
      case ChoreRecurrence.Yearly:
        return 'bg-teal-100 text-teal-700';
      default:
        if (schedule) return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700';
        return 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600';
    }
  };

  const label = getLabel();
  if (!label) return null;

  return (
    &lt;div className={`bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 text-xs font-semibold px-2 py-1 rounded-full flex items-center space-x-1 ${getColorClass()}`}&gt;
      {getIcon()}
      &lt;span&gt;{label}&lt;/span&gt;
    &lt;/div&gt;
  );
};

const ExtraChoreBadge: React.FC = () =&gt; (
  &lt;div className="bg-pink-100 text-pink-700 text-xs font-semibold px-2 py-1 rounded-full flex items-center space-x-1"&gt;
    &lt;StarIcon className="w-3 h-3" /&gt;
    &lt;span&gt;Extra Chore&lt;/span&gt;
  &lt;/div&gt;
);

const VarianceBadge: React.FC&lt;{ schedule?: ChoreSchedule }&gt; = ({ schedule }) =&gt; {
  if (!schedule || schedule.type !== 'DEADLINE' || !schedule.varianceDays) return null;
  return (
    &lt;div className="bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 text-xs font-semibold px-2 py-1 rounded-full flex items-center space-x-1"&gt;
      &lt;ClockIcon className="w-3 h-3" /&gt;
      &lt;span&gt;Complete by {schedule.value} (±{schedule.varianceDays} days)&lt;/span&gt;
    &lt;/div&gt;
  );
};

export const isWithinVarianceWindow = (schedule: ChoreSchedule | undefined): boolean =&gt; {
  if (!schedule || schedule.type !== 'DEADLINE' || !schedule.value) return true;
  const dueDate = new Date(schedule.value);
  dueDate.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  const today = new Date();
  today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  const varianceMs = (schedule.varianceDays || 0) * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  const startWindow = new Date(dueDate.getTime() - varianceMs);
  const endWindow = new Date(dueDate.getTime() + varianceMs);
  return today &gt;= startWindow &amp;&amp; today &lt;= endWindow;
};

const ChoreCard: React.FC&lt;ChoreCardProps&gt; = ({ chore, onStateChange, currentUser, onEdit, onOverride }) =&gt; {
  const [isDescriptionVisible, setIsDescriptionVisible] = useState(false);
  const [showDenyModal, setShowDenyModal] = useState(false);

  const cardStyles = {
    [ChoreStatus.Incomplete]: 'bg-white',
    [ChoreStatus.PendingApproval]: 'bg-amber-50',
    [ChoreStatus.Completed]: 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-500',
    [ChoreStatus.RetryRequested]: 'bg-blue-50',
  };

  const titleStyles = {
    [ChoreStatus.Incomplete]: '',
    [ChoreStatus.PendingApproval]: 'text-amber-800',
    [ChoreStatus.Completed]: 'line-through',
    [ChoreStatus.RetryRequested]: 'text-blue-800',
  };

  const handleCompleteClick = () =&gt; {
    if (chore.schedule &amp;&amp; !isWithinVarianceWindow(chore.schedule)) {
      if (chore.schedule.type === 'DEADLINE') {
        const dueDate = new Date(chore.schedule.value);
        dueDate.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
        const variance = chore.schedule.varianceDays || 0;
        const start = new Date(dueDate.getTime() - variance * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
        const end = new Date(dueDate.getTime() + variance * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
        alert(`This chore must be completed between ${start.toLocaleDateString()} and ${end.toLocaleDateString()}`);
        return;
      }
    }
    const nextStatus = chore.requiresApproval ? ChoreStatus.PendingApproval : ChoreStatus.Completed;
    onStateChange(chore.id, nextStatus);
  };

  const handleApproveClick = () =&gt; {
    if (chore.schedule &amp;&amp; !isWithinVarianceWindow(chore.schedule)) {
      if (chore.schedule.type === 'DEADLINE') {
        const dueDate = new Date(chore.schedule.value);
        dueDate.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
        const variance = chore.schedule.varianceDays || 0;
        const start = new Date(dueDate.getTime() - variance * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
        const end = new Date(dueDate.getTime() + variance * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
        alert(`Can only approve within the completion window (${start.toLocaleDateString()} - ${end.toLocaleDateString()})`);
        return;
      }
    }
    onStateChange(chore.id, ChoreStatus.Completed);
  };

  const handleDenyClick = () =&gt; {
    setShowDenyModal(true);
  };

  const handleDenyAndRemove = () =&gt; {
    setShowDenyModal(false);
    onStateChange(chore.id, ChoreStatus.Incomplete);
  };

  const handleDenyAndRetry = () =&gt; {
    setShowDenyModal(false);
    onStateChange(chore.id, ChoreStatus.RetryRequested);
  };

  return (
    &lt;div className={`relative transition-all duration-300 rounded-xl shadow-lg flex flex-col p-4 ${cardStyles[chore.status]} ${chore.isExtraChore ? 'ring-2 ring-pink-300' : ''}`}&gt;
      &lt;div className="absolute top-3 right-3 flex flex-col items-end space-y-1"&gt;
        &lt;RecurrenceBadge recurrence={chore.recurrence} schedule={chore.schedule} /&gt;
        {chore.isExtraChore &amp;&amp; &lt;ExtraChoreBadge /&gt;}
        &lt;VarianceBadge schedule={chore.schedule} /&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div className="flex-grow"&gt;
        &lt;div className="flex items-start justify-between pr-2"&gt;
           &lt;h3 className={`font-bold text-lg pr-2 ${titleStyles[chore.status]}`}&gt;{chore.name}&lt;/h3&gt;
            &lt;div className="flex items-center space-x-2 flex-shrink-0"&gt;
               {chore.description &amp;&amp; (
                &lt;button onClick={() =&gt; setIsDescriptionVisible(!isDescriptionVisible)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-sky-500"&gt;
                  &lt;InformationCircleIcon className="w-6 h-6" /&gt;
                &lt;/button&gt;
              )}
              {currentUser.role === 'parent' &amp;&amp; (
                &lt;button onClick={() =&gt; onEdit(chore)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-sky-600"&gt;
                  &lt;PencilIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;
                &lt;/button&gt;
              )}
           &lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;/div&gt;

        {isDescriptionVisible &amp;&amp; chore.description &amp;&amp; (
          &lt;p className="text-sm text-slate-600 mt-2 bg-slate-100 p-2 rounded-md"&gt;{chore.description}&lt;/p&gt;
        )}

        &lt;div className="flex items-center space-x-4 mt-2"&gt;
          &lt;div className="flex items-center space-x-1 text-amber-500"&gt;
            &lt;StarIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;
            &lt;span className="font-semibold text-lg"&gt;{chore.points} Points&lt;/span&gt;
          &lt;/div&gt;
          {chore.streak &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; (
            &lt;div className="flex items-center space-x-1 text-orange-500 font-semibold" title={`${chore.streak} day streak`}&gt;
              &lt;FireIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;
              &lt;span className="text-lg"&gt;{chore.streak}&lt;/span&gt;
            &lt;/div&gt;
          )}
        &lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;

      &lt;div className="mt-4"&gt;
        {chore.status === ChoreStatus.Incomplete &amp;&amp; (
          &lt;&gt;
            {currentUser.role === 'parent' &amp;&amp; chore.assignedTo ? (
              &lt;button
                onClick={() =&gt; onOverride(chore.id)}
                className="w-full font-bold py-2 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors bg-indigo-500 text-white hover:bg-indigo-600 flex items-center justify-center space-x-2"
                aria-label="Override and mark as complete"
              &gt;
                &lt;ShieldCheckIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;
                &lt;span&gt;Override Completion&lt;/span&gt;
              &lt;/button&gt;
            ) : (
              &lt;button
                onClick={handleCompleteClick}
                className="w-full font-bold py-2 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors bg-sky-500 text-white hover:bg-sky-600"
              &gt;
                Mark as Done
              &lt;/button&gt;
            )}
          &lt;/&gt;
        )}

        {chore.status === ChoreStatus.PendingApproval &amp;&amp; (
          &lt;div className="text-center"&gt;
              &lt;p className="text-amber-700 font-semibold mb-2 text-sm"&gt;Awaiting Approval&lt;/p&gt;
              {currentUser.role === 'parent' &amp;&amp; (
                  &lt;div className="flex space-x-2"&gt;
                    &lt;button
                        onClick={handleDenyClick}
                        className="flex-1 font-bold py-2 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-300"
                    &gt;
                        Deny
                    &lt;/button&gt;
                    &lt;button
                        onClick={handleApproveClick}
                        className="flex-1 font-bold py-2 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors bg-amber-500 text-white hover:bg-amber-600"
                    &gt;
                        Approve
                    &lt;/button&gt;
                  &lt;/div&gt;
              )}
          &lt;/div&gt;
        )}

        {chore.status === ChoreStatus.RetryRequested &amp;&amp; (
          &lt;div className="text-center"&gt;
            &lt;p className="text-blue-700 font-semibold mb-2 text-sm"&gt;Retry Requested&lt;/p&gt;
            {currentUser.role === 'parent' &amp;&amp; (
              &lt;div className="flex space-x-2"&gt;
                &lt;button
                  onClick={() =&gt; onStateChange(chore.id, ChoreStatus.Incomplete)}
                  className="flex-1 font-bold py-2 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-300"
                &gt;
                  Cancel Retry
                &lt;/button&gt;
                &lt;button
                  onClick={() =&gt; onStateChange(chore.id, ChoreStatus.Completed)}
                  className="flex-1 font-bold py-2 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors bg-blue-500 text-white hover:bg-blue-600"
                &gt;
                  Approve Retry
                &lt;/button&gt;
              &lt;/div&gt;
            )}
            {currentUser.role === 'child' &amp;&amp; (
              &lt;button
                onClick={handleCompleteClick}
                className="w-full font-bold py-2 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors bg-blue-500 text-white hover:bg-blue-600"
              &gt;
                Complete (Retry)
              &lt;/button&gt;
            )}
          &lt;/div&gt;
        )}

        {chore.status === ChoreStatus.Completed &amp;&amp; (
          &lt;button
            disabled
            className="w-full font-bold py-2 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors bg-green-500 text-white cursor-not-allowed"
          &gt;
            &lt;div className="flex items-center justify-center space-x-2"&gt;
              &lt;CheckCircleIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;
              &lt;span&gt;Done!&lt;/span&gt;
            &lt;/div&gt;
          &lt;/button&gt;
        )}
      &lt;/div&gt;

        &lt;div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 z-50 flex justify-center items-center p-4" onClick={() =&gt; setShowDenyModal(false)}&gt;
          &lt;div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-6 w-full max-w-sm" onClick={e =&gt; e.stopPropagation()}&gt;
            &lt;div className="text-center"&gt;
              &lt;XCircleIcon className="w-12 h-12 text-red-500 mx-auto mb-3" /&gt;
              &lt;h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800 mb-2"&gt;Deny Chore&lt;/h3&gt;
              &lt;p className="text-slate-600 mb-6"&gt;
                How would you like to deny &lt;strong&gt;"{chore.name}"&lt;/strong&gt;?
              &lt;/p&gt;
              &lt;div className="space-y-3"&gt;
                &lt;button
                  onClick={handleDenyAndRemove}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-300 transition-colors"
                &gt;
                  Deny &amp; Remove
                &lt;/button&gt;
                &lt;button
                  onClick={handleDenyAndRetry}
                  className="w-full bg-blue-500 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-600 transition-colors"
                &gt;
                  Deny &amp; Retry
                &lt;/button&gt;
              &lt;/div&gt;
            &lt;/div&gt;
          &lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;/div&gt;
      )}
    &lt;/div&gt;
  );
};

export default ChoreCard;

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { PointRequest, PointRequestStatus } from '../types';
import { StarIcon, CheckCircleIcon } from './icons';

interface PointRequestCardProps {
  request: PointRequest;
  onAction: (id: number, newStatus: PointRequestStatus.Approved | PointRequestStatus.Denied, adjustedPoints?: number) =&gt; void;
  userName?: string;
}

const PointRequestCard: React.FC&lt;PointRequestCardProps&gt; = ({ request, onAction, userName }) =&gt; {
  const [showAdjustModal, setShowAdjustModal] = useState(false);
  const [adjustedPoints, setAdjustedPoints] = useState(request.points.toString());
  
  const cardBgColor = {
    [PointRequestStatus.Pending]: 'bg-white',
    [PointRequestStatus.Approved]: 'bg-green-50',
    [PointRequestStatus.Denied]: 'bg-red-50',
  };

  const statusBadge = {
    [PointRequestStatus.Pending]: &lt;span className="text-xs font-semibold px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-amber-200 text-amber-800"&gt;Pending&lt;/span&gt;,
    [PointRequestStatus.Approved]: &lt;span className="text-xs font-semibold px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-green-200 text-green-800"&gt;Approved&lt;/span&gt;,
    [PointRequestStatus.Denied]: &lt;span className="text-xs font-semibold px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-red-200 text-red-800"&gt;Denied&lt;/span&gt;,
  }

  const handleApproveLess = () =&gt; {
    const pts = parseInt(adjustedPoints, 10);
    if (!isNaN(pts) &amp;&amp; pts &gt;= 0) {
      onAction(request.id, PointRequestStatus.Approved, pts);
      setShowAdjustModal(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    &lt;div className={`transition-all duration-300 rounded-xl shadow-lg flex flex-col p-4 ${cardBgColor[request.status]}`}&gt;
      &lt;div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2"&gt;
        &lt;div className="flex-grow pr-4"&gt;
            {userName &amp;&amp; &lt;p className="font-bold text-slate-800"&gt;{userName}'s Request&lt;/p&gt;}
            &lt;p className="text-slate-600 text-sm italic"&gt;"{request.description}"&lt;/p&gt;
        &lt;/div&gt;
        {statusBadge[request.status]}
      &lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;div className="flex items-center space-x-1 text-amber-500 mt-2 mb-4"&gt;
        &lt;StarIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;
        &lt;span className="font-semibold text-lg"&gt;{request.points} Points Requested&lt;/span&gt;
      &lt;/div&gt;
      
      {request.status === PointRequestStatus.Pending &amp;&amp; (
        &lt;div className="space-y-2"&gt;
          &lt;div className="flex space-x-2"&gt;
            &lt;button
              onClick={() =&gt; onAction(request.id, PointRequestStatus.Denied)}
              className="flex-1 font-bold py-2 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-300"
            &gt;
              Deny
            &lt;/button&gt;
            &lt;button
              onClick={() =&gt; onAction(request.id, PointRequestStatus.Approved)}
              className="flex-1 font-bold py-2 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors bg-green-500 text-white hover:bg-green-600"
            &gt;
              Approve
            &lt;/button&gt;
          &lt;/div&gt;
          &lt;button
            onClick={() =&gt; setShowAdjustModal(true)}
            className="w-full font-bold py-2 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 hover:bg-amber-200 text-sm"
          &gt;
            Approve Less
          &lt;/button&gt;
        &lt;/div&gt;
      )}

      {showAdjustModal &amp;&amp; (
        &lt;div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 z-50 flex justify-center items-center p-4" onClick={() =&gt; setShowAdjustModal(false)}&gt;
          &lt;div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-6 w-full max-w-sm" onClick={e =&gt; e.stopPropagation()}&gt;
            &lt;h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800 mb-2"&gt;Approve Less&lt;/h3&gt;
            &lt;p className="text-slate-600 mb-4"&gt;
              The child requested &lt;strong&gt;{request.points} points&lt;/strong&gt;. How many would you like to award?
            &lt;/p&gt;
            &lt;input
              type="number"
              value={adjustedPoints}
              onChange={e =&gt; setAdjustedPoints(e.target.value)}
              min="0"
              max={request.points}
              className="w-full p-3 border rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-slate-800 mb-4"
              autoFocus
            /&gt;
            &lt;div className="flex space-x-2"&gt;
              &lt;button
                onClick={() =&gt; setShowAdjustModal(false)}
                className="flex-1 bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-300 transition-colors"
              &gt;
                Cancel
              &lt;/button&gt;
              &lt;button
                onClick={handleApproveLess}
                className="flex-1 bg-amber-500 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-amber-600 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center space-x-1"
              &gt;
                &lt;CheckCircleIcon className="w-5 h-5" /&gt;
                &lt;span&gt;Award {adjustedPoints || 0}&lt;/span&gt;
              &lt;/button&gt;
            &lt;/div&gt;
          &lt;/div&gt;
        &lt;/div&gt;
      )}
    &lt;/div&gt;
  );
};

export default PointRequestCard;

</dyad-write>

## Key Decisions Made
- **File structure is flat, not `src/`**: Discovered that `App.tsx`, `types.ts`, etc. live in root, while components are in `components/`.
- **Use localStorage for session persistence**: Chosen over cookies since the app already relies on localStorage for all state, and localStorage satisfies mobile persistence needs for this client-side app.
- **PIN range is 4–8 digits**: Defined for child authentication with a visual dot indicator on the login screen.
- **Activity events tracked explicitly**: Created `ActivityEvent` type and `ActivityEventType` enum to log all chore/reward/point changes for the Activity Log view.

## Code Changes Completed
- `types.ts` — Added `ActivityEvent`, `ActivityEventType`, `ChildPIN`, `ChoreSchedule`, `ChoreScheduleType`, `ChoreStatus.RetryRequested`, extended `ChoreRecurrence` with `Monthly`/`Yearly`, and added `isExtraChore`/`schedule`/`retryAllowed` to `Chore`.
- `components/icons.tsx` — Added `XCircleIcon`, `ClockIcon`, `CalendarIcon`, `ArrowDownIcon`, `ListIcon`, `LockIcon`, `ArrowLeftIcon`, `QuestionMarkCircleIcon` to support new UI features.
- `components/LoginScreen.tsx` — **New** child PIN entry screen with numeric keypad, backspace, 4–8 digit validation, error state, and “Forgot PIN” helper modal.
- `components/ActivityLog.tsx` — **New** parent-facing activity log with child and event-type dropdown filters, colored event badges, and 100-event limit.
- `components/ChoreCard.tsx` — Added `RecurrenceBadge`, `ExtraChoreBadge`, `VarianceBadge`; implemented `isWithinVarianceWindow` helper; added “Deny &amp; Remove” vs “Deny &amp; Retry” modal; styled retry-requested and extra-chore states.
- `components/PointRequestCard.tsx` — Added “Approve Less” button that opens a modal to award a custom point amount (≤ requested) instead of full approval.

## Current Task State
The assistant was about to perform a major rewrite of `App.tsx` to wire together all new features (PIN auth, activity logging, schedule forms, extra chores, enhanced approval states, session management, and the Activity Log tab). They had just re-read `App.tsx` and `LoginScreen.tsx` to verify current state before writing.

## Active Plan
**Plan:** Complete Chore System with Activity Log &amp; PIN Authentication  
**Status:** Partially implemented — supporting components and types are done; the main `App.tsx` integration and likely `Header.tsx` updates remain.  
**Key implementation steps remaining:**
1. Rewrite `App.tsx` to add new state (`activityLog`, `childPINs`, schedule form fields, session/auth flags).
2. Integrate `LoginScreen` and session persistence logic into `App.tsx`.
3. Update chore add/edit handlers to support `schedule`, `isExtraChore`, and monthly/yearly options.
4. Wire `handleChoreStateChange` to log `ActivityEvent`s and enforce variance windows.
5. Add Activity Log tab to navigation and render `ActivityLog` component for parents.
6. Update `Header.tsx` if needed for child-friendly view and tab visibility logic.

## Important Context
- **No build errors or runtime errors surfaced yet** — the app was mid-implementation.
- **Session/auth pattern planned**: Check `localStorage` for saved session on load; child logs in via PIN through `LoginScreen`; parent uses “I’ll login as parent” bypass; save session on successful login.
- **Variance window logic**: `isWithinVarianceWindow` in `ChoreCard.tsx` checks whether today falls within `deadline ± varianceDays` before allowing completion/approval.
- The app uses `useLocalStorage` hook for all persistent state and Tailwind CSS via CDN.
- Existing views/tabs in `App.tsx` before rewrite: Chores, Rewards, Requests, Users. Activity Log needs to be added as a new parent-visible view.

## Standing Preferences &amp; Constraints
- **No new dependencies without asking** (implied by existing setup using only React + Tailwind CDN).
- Tailwind utility classes only (no CSS files) — consistent with current component styling approach.
